The Bank of Slovenia has a reduced competence with regard to the prudential supervision of business banks, since to a certain extent it is directly assumed by the European Central Bank. Banka Slovenije also takes over the bank resolution fund. By adopting the Banking Act 2, it adopts a system for the exchange of information on client creditworthiness. Finally, today it is difficult to predict the impact of the European Union directive on the Bank of Slovenia on the monetary union. It is in line with the SMC's position that the proposal for a law amending and supplementing the Banka Slovenije Act is not suitable for further consideration.

At the same time, the law adds a new provision in Article 12 that it is also mandatory to define ways of integrating patriotism into the educational and educational programmes themselves. At the same time, with a substantive change, we also want to amend Article 86, which provides for the financing of private schools. Private schools are now funded at 85% and the Constitutional Court is by decision 4. 12. 2013 provides that this Article is unconstitutional and that private schools must be financed in the same way as public schools are financed. We therefore propose to amend the second paragraph of this Article so that private schools are financed by 100%. ParlaMint-SI_2015-03-31-SDZ7-Redna-06.ana.u240	Dear Presidents, honourable Members and Members of Parliament! Allow me to briefly present the harmonisation amendment to Articles 53 and 402 before the third reading of the draft Banking Act. The Government of the Amendment shall submit, pursuant to Article 140 of the Rules of Procedure of the National Assembly, due to the mutual non-compliance of the individual provisions of the Law due to the amendments adopted so far in the procedure for the examination of the draft law. The amendment to Article 53 is due to the non-conformity of the legal text which was the result of the amendment adopted to Article 54 of the draft Banking Act. In the second paragraph, it contained a period within which the candidate for a member of the supervisory board of the bank had to comply with the requirements laid down in Article 36, which lays down the conditions for the simultaneous exercise of several managerial functions. ParlaMint-SI_2015-03-31-SDZ7-Redna-06.ana.u277	Thank you for your word, President. Dear Members and Members of the State, the Secretary of State has another nice greeting! The main purpose of the amendments to banking legislation is mainly the transposition of EU legislation prepared in connection with and response to the recent financial crisis. The proposal for a law bearing the ZBan-2 code lays down the conditions for the establishment, operation, termination by new credit institutions, means banks and savings banks, powers and procedures for the exercise of control over the operations of banks and savings banks and the powers and procedures for the management of systemic risks related to the credit institutions themselves operating in the Republic of Slovenia. Surely there are risks and capital among important areas. As regards risk management, commonly known as liquidity and credit, this law extends to eight categories of risks themselves. In parallel, the implementation of internal controls, as well as the Bank's Compliance Institute, should be defined. No law, which is so good, makes it possible to do good banking if it is not followed in the first place by the most responsible in the banking management process by the right management board and the supervisory board. This is followed by responsible owners and ultimately by a bank regulator. In the SMC, we expect credit institutions to operate responsibly and in accordance with the principle of a good expert, and so on. to restore confidence in the banking system, especially in state-owned banks. However, we expect the regulator of the Bank of Slovenia to exercise full competence and responsibility and to take measures against managers in credit institutions in a timely and decisive manner so that the period 2008-2013 does not recur. The Bank of Slovenia must ensure banking operations in order to operate as expected by savings agents, commissioners and users of banking services, and last but not least the European Central Bank. In the SMC, we believe that the proposed law provides a good basis, so we will support ZBan-2. Thank you very much.
